  adapter registry for speculative corpora; later values remain additive.
- Scope the "sole surface-facing search" claim to **conversation** search and
  keep `search_memory` as the claim-scoped memory entry point (LD 58).
- Exclude assistant turns carrying the deterministic Search-result render marker
  from projection so returned snippets do not recursively become search corpus.
- Default Search on for local Web/CLI/TUI (LD 60), with mapped DM and E2EE
  still behind their grants, and state the asymmetry with default-off Memory
  collection wherever both appear.
- Implement safe term/phrase/prefix/filter parsing, BM25/tie-break sorting,
  bounded snippets/limits, generation/revision-bound cursor, bounded
  post-authorization overfetch/refill, scanned/filtered/incomplete metadata,
  freshness, and `search_not_ready`. Cursor position is the last scanned total-
  order tuple so omitted leading candidates neither loop nor skip later hits.
- Add query-time Corpus reauthorization and the optional trace-safe Runner
  summary hook. It runs before `action_requested`, governs completion/error
  summaries, and omits values on callback failure; raw query/snippet/filter
  operands never enter durable confirmation/resume state.
- Add registered `authorize_search_query_scope` with `resumable?: true` to the
  existing explicit generic-resume allowlist. Its safe params are principal,
  exact origin tuple, scope, expiry, source message id, safe filter shape, and
  non-secret key ref/version plus keyed request binding; it returns
  `query_resubmit_required`, uses the resolved
  confirmation id as `query_chain_id`, and stores no raw query or second grant.
  Client resubmission carries the transient query and confirmation id; Search
  recomputes/matches the binding. Canonical delete and Search purge are separate
  resumable allowlisted actions.

Acceptance:

- No raw MATCH/query/snippet reaches logs/audits; injection-shaped input is
  rejected or escaped by the typed grammar.
- Secret/hidden/tool/model/system/action-log fixtures never enter any current,
  previous, build-temporary, failed, retired, or pending-prune Search DB/
  sidecar state, and audit filter operands are absent.
- Stale/deleted/revoked/digest-mismatched candidates are omitted before a
  snippet/result is returned and cause bounded repair work.
- Leading stale/unauthorized candidates are scanned past in bounded batches
  until page full/EOF/budget, and cursor continuation neither repeats nor skips
  the next authorized result.
- Failed rebuild preserves the prior verified generation; incompatible cursor
  or projection revision restarts rather than mixing pages; promotion proves
  checkpoint `busy = 0`, self-contained proof, bounded serving-handle drain/
  close, swap, verified current reopen, and no required sidecar; timeout/failure
  keeps the previous generation, no renamed live handle survives, and no
  canonical scan fallback exists.
- Duplicate source ingestion updates one locator/FTS row; delete/reconcile uses
  the unique locator without scanning FTS, and integrity rejects any missing,
  duplicate, or mismatched rowid pair.
- Repair/rebuild records starting population high-water/revision plus schema,
  tokenizer, redactor, and eligibility-epoch/configuration versions; sweep
  cannot remove later writes,
  promotion preserves any dirty work newer than its final applied high-water,
  and incompatible policy change/purge invalidates resumable builders/cursors.

#### M7 — Search managed ingestion, reconcile, maintenance, delete, and purge

`[sqlite]` `[gate]`

Status: **implementation complete 2026-07-29** at the focused lifecycle rejoin
recorded in Build Progress; surface/privacy/eval and packaged-target acceptance
remain assigned to M8/M9.

- Reconcile `search-index`, `search-maintain`, and `search-rebuild` through
  Jobs.Managed and `:search_manage`.
- Implement dirty marking plus `Jobs.Managed.kick/2` of `search-index`, hourly
  repair, bounded weekly integrity/
  stale-row/generation prune/incremental merge, resumable rebuild, canonical
  delete reconciliation, and separately confirmed secure purge. Jobs owns
  coalescing/pause/run history and invokes the registered action through the
  Runner; no Search timer/process invokes it directly.
- Treat `search-rebuild` with nil due time as active/manual, distinct from a
  paused row. Reconcile kicks it when no compatible verified generation exists
  after first boot, restore, or schema/tokenizer/redactor-policy change;
  incomplete runs become due with bounded recoverable backoff and a missing
  SQLite/FTS capability reports degraded state. The atomic admission/completion
  rule from M2 prevents overlap and lost kicks across all three entries.
- Implement `purge_search_projection` as a resumable allowlisted action over a
  content-free out-of-generation control record carrying non-secret key ref/
  version plus the opaque bound scope. Quiesce every Search operation
  except the same continuation while a phase is incomplete; queries/jobs return
  `search_purge_in_progress`, kicks coalesce, and pause state survives.
- Register every Search DB/sidecar as derived and excluded from authoritative
  Home export/backup; restore starts without it and rebuilds from Corpus.
- Keep canonical conversation retention unchanged.

Acceptance:

- Normal ingestion reaches the M1 freshness target without delaying canonical
  writes; pause suppresses kicks without clearing dirty state, and resume or a
  missed wakeup is repaired through the managed hourly path.
- Repeated reconcile creates no duplicate jobs or index rows.
- Restore or missing/incompatible-generation bootstrap schedules exactly one
  rebuild without conflating active-manual with pause; incomplete work retries
  with bounded backoff, capability failure degrades, and a completion-race kick
  remains due.
- Maintenance is bounded and does not run full optimize/VACUUM routinely.
- Purge denies still-eligible content, then proves every current/previous/temp/
  failed/retired/pending-prune Search DB and sidecar has no openable or
  startup-recoverable purge fixture; historical-delete generations are rebuilt
  or deleted wholesale under the documented narrow claim. Purge first persists
  recovery phase and invalidates all builders/cursors; every other Search
  operation is quiesced with `search_purge_in_progress`, and restart completes
  or remains fail-closed rather than reopening pre-purge resumable state.
- Authoritative export/backup contains no Search DB/sidecar, and restore
  reconstructs Search only through the normal Corpus rebuild path.

#### M8 — Search surfaces, privacy, cross-consumer rejoin, and evals

`[gate]` `[external]`

- All surfaces return equivalent source-linked results for equal authorized

- DM elevation matches its keyed request/scope binding, expires, paginates

#### M9.b.3 — Source operator-validation and intent-selection remediation

`[code]` `[docs]` `[gate]` `[audit]` `[external]` `[fv]`

- Treat Engine/classifier and Router execute/clarify outcomes as proposals at
  the central Engine API and every effectful Jido consumer, including
  `IntentAgent` and the Objectives default lifecycle. Engine must not synthesize
  `:registry_action` or a non-`nil` selected action from an ordinary false- or
  unresolved-evidence candidate. Add a validated, additive descriptor selection
  policy with
  `:semantic` as the schema-compatibility default, not an unconditional accept.
  A semantic model/ranker proposal requires a positive descriptor-owned
  operator-act phrase at the start after bounded polite scaffolding; a
  contiguous phrase elsewhere and the broader ordered/fuzzy ranking match are
  not final evidence. Wrapped, negated, referential, and supplied-text mentions
  reject. Unicode letters and numbers form operator-language tokens;
  punctuation, symbols, and emoji are boundaries for positive phrases,
  negative vetoes, and clarification categories. Required slots alone authorize
  only when a descriptor explicitly sets
  validated `vocabulary.allow_required_slot_selection: true`, default `false`.
  No current v1.3 descriptor opts in unless the frozen-corpus compatibility
  check identifies, and review records, a required case.
- Use `:explicit_evidence` for actions such as Memory append/read that must not
  be inferred from quoted or supplied action language. Descriptor-owned action
  language must lead the operator turn after only bounded polite scaffolding;
  extracted slots never independently authorize it. Resolve the active policy
  canonically by proposed action identity at the Jido boundary, fail closed when
  it cannot be resolved, and preserve/expose it across descriptor override,
  edit, promotion, and inspection paths. Advisory model diagnostics cannot
  weaken it. An explicit deterministic `route_decision`/route hint is already
  selected by the typed ladder, so Engine annotates rather than replaces it; a
  resolved semantic predicate remains route evidence, while explicit-policy
  ladder routes obey the same stricter consumer rule. This exception never
  authorizes an ordinary ranked/model candidate.
- Strictly validate a proposed clarification as one complete proper shortlist;
  one malformed option rejects it all. Reduce a valid shortlist to canonical
  grounded options before rendering or persistence. Dedicated descriptor
  category vocabulary may ground presentation only. A reply must resolve one
  persisted option, which is revalidated against the original prompt through
  the current canonical clarification contract before the normal parameter,
  Registry, Runner, Security Central, and confirmation gates. A shortlist with
  no supported option uses the clean direct-answer path. If the chosen action is
  missing required parameters, persist its normal parameter clarification and
  accept those values on the operator's third turn before continuing that same
  bound action through the normal gates.
- Treat a model/Router `:none` as advisory abstention. Ordinary input takes a
  fresh validated `IntentAgent` direct-answer decision; only the canonical
  deterministic `InputGuard` may retain terminal no-match for slash-command or
  internal-action-shaped input, and advisory diagnostics cannot impersonate it.
  A rejected or malformed proposal follows the consumer's clean canonical path
  (`IntentAgent` decision or Objectives action step), carries no rejected slots/
  resource metadata, creates no rejected action or pending clarification, and
  cannot be reinterpreted as another Engine action. Registry, Runner, Security
  Central, confirmation, and surface contracts remain unchanged.
- Under the default `:two_stage_local` strategy, keep one model selection owner
  rather than invoking the legacy classifier before the Router. Preserve the
  deterministic strategy, and apply the applicable final proposal-acceptance
  rule to either path. Reuse the execute/clarify rule in deterministic intent
  evaluation, with an explicit reproducible availability mode that includes
  capability/demo-gated descriptors while honoring operator-disabled overrides.
  Eval parity is proposal-selection parity, not a claim that replay proves every
  interactive consumer outcome.
- Add human-reviewed committed Memory boundary cases for explicit remember/
  recall requests and quoted action language. Keep the exact failed supplied-
  text prompts in final IntentAgent behavior tests and real-provider source FV:
  the frozen Stage-1 fake selector must not gain prompt-specific logic merely to
  make those whole-turn prompts fit the deterministic corpus. Recapture the
  committed baseline only after the complete corpus passes at `1.0`; never lower
  or bypass the gate to absorb a failure.
- Compose every core single-turn ReqLLM chat/object request through one
  provenance-aware prompt envelope. Allbert-owned declarative rules use the
  system role; the typed task input is the final user message; reviewed Memory,
  candidates, recent context, and registered metadata stay user-role data.
  DirectAnswer uses operator text as its final task input and attaches images
  only there. Keep already-correct specialized multi-turn/Jido.AI paths separate
  and exclude non-chat APIs. Derive DirectAnswer instructions from one generic
  rule catalog rather than duplicating prompt prose. Do not add a prompt-specific
  output matcher, automatic retry, or model judge unless attended evidence
  proves the structural correction insufficient.
- Qualify DirectAnswer independently of the global first-model substrate. Keep
  global `local` and the curated row on `llama3.2:3b`; add
  `direct_answer_local` on `qwen2.5:7b` with temperature `0`, maximum `1024`
  output tokens, and a 60-second timeout. Force deterministic temperature at
  the DirectAnswer request boundary without changing unrelated consumers.
  Default the task preference to `["direct_answer_local"]`.
- Treat a non-empty DirectAnswer task list as a closed chain in authored order:
  never append/reorder the global primary or admit a merely configured hosted
  provider. Preserve empty-list primary compatibility. Keep `admin models use`
  as the intentional broad switch that updates global and DirectAnswer, while

- Add one thin source-checkout dispatcher, `mix allbert <argv>`, over the unified
  CLI boundary. Ordinary commands must attempt daemon attachment before any

  session plus second-terminal `mix allbert ...` commands. Do not construct or

- Classify a source finding as environment, documentation, or executable. Repeat
  an environment row after setup repair; validate a docs-only correction with

  tranche with focused tests per commit. An executable SHA change closes the old

Acceptance:

- In focused Jido-boundary and Objectives-consumer tests the two exact supplied-
  text Project Juniper prompts receive clean direct-answer outcomes: they cannot

- Structural adapter tests prove that Allbert rules, reviewed/retrieved data,
  operator content, and vision attachments retain their canonical roles and
  order across every core single-turn model adapter. In attended validation,

- Settings/schema/catalog tests prove the qualified profile's exact provider,

- Engine-focused tests prove that ordinary false- or unresolved-evidence

- `mix allbert serve`, `mix allbert tui`, `mix allbert ask`, `mix allbert search`,

- Focused intent, eval, dispatcher, warning-free compile, targeted static, docs,

#### M9.b.4 — Manager-planned fan-out and selective Jido workers

`[code]` `[docs]` `[gate]` `[audit]` `[external]`

- Remove broad regex/list-shape admission as the authority for implicit fan-out.
  Preserve steering, Search/notification special forms, nested-fan-out refusal,
  and the two exact counted offline protocols. A turn that reaches the clean
  DirectAnswer route uses one qualified conversation-model call to return
  either the ordinary answer or a parallel-work proposal. This is a private
  manager Interface, not a registered action, intent candidate, permission,
  confirmation outcome, or execution route. v1.3 does not replace the existing
  action router: uncounted mixed/effectful compound work remains single, while
  the exact counted protocol is the explicit force path for such work.
  Malformed/partial/count-mismatched exact protocols return a typed manager-off
  single-turn outcome; they cannot fall through to adaptive planning.
- Add one typed plan Interface and deterministic compiler. The bounded model
  assessment contains one useful answer, explicit closed evidence for every
  admission rule, and only ordered children with title, objective, and expected
  result; the compiler assigns plan version/source. A private Jido Agent owns
  the `assess -> adjudicate` lifecycle, but adjudication is deterministic local
  policy, not a routine second provider call. One declarative rule catalog
  derives the model instructions and local adjudication criteria for semantic
  independence, supplied-text ownership, advisory/read-only scope, coverage,
  child-result dependency, parent-only join guidance, and useful parallel
  leverage. The compiler then validates the closed schema, original-request
  binding, bounds, non-empty unique children, and absence of forbidden
  action/agent/authority fields. Permit at most one bounded repair provider call
  only after malformed or internally inconsistent evidence; an ordinary valid
  assessment plus local adjudication consumes exactly one provider call.
  Unavailable, malformed, dependent, uncertain, or still-invalid proposals use
  the answer returned by the same assessment or fail closed to the ordinary
  DirectAnswer path; overflow retains the existing complete-list clarification.
  Counted protocols enter the same compiler without a model.
- Treat the parent objective plus its ordered child rows as the canonical plan;
  store only compact version/source/digest provenance in existing bounded
  metadata and the `fanout_proposed` event. Do not duplicate the full plan in a
  new JSON column. Worker selection happens deterministically after the existing
  Engine proposal is validated: model output cannot select an action, agent
  module, worker kind, permission, identity, dependency, confirmation result,
  or delivery route. Admission uses the uncapped scoped `active_parent/2`
  lookup and immediate-transaction `frame_if_inactive/2`, so awaiting, running,
  or recovering work and same-scope concurrent requests cannot create a second
  active parent.
- Deepen child execution behind one Worker Interface with two Adapters. Preserve
  current Registry/Runner Lifecycle behavior for non-DirectAnswer actions;
  execute a clean DirectAnswer child through a temporary bounded Jido worker
  that delegates to that same registered DirectAnswer action and therefore
  reuses its model resolution, disclosure, Active Memory, prompt envelope,
  redaction, and failure policy. Give a worker only its child-scoped original
  context and explicit budgets. An unchanged digest-verified child receives a
  bounded Allbert-authored DirectAnswer envelope containing its objective and
  expected-result guidance; the guidance grants no action authority. Validate
  effect evidence against the original operator turn, never generated child
  prose alone. Conversation-manager
  children are DirectAnswer-only. For an exact-counted child, both a fresh and
  recovered persisted action/params packet must revalidate against its digest-
  verified exact operator span before execution. This v1.3 child selection and
  validation is deterministic/model-off; legacy ordinary Objectives retain
  their compatible model-assist path. A registered action may normalize the
  params it stores for confirmation resume; park that action-owned packet's
  canonical digest on the durable step, then recheck it at approval and again
  immediately before Runner instead of either demanding equality with the
  original params or trusting mutable confirmation YAML. Missing/inconsistent v1.3 provenance
  fails closed and cannot inherit the legacy retry/budget allowance. Every
  effect still crosses Registry, Runner, Security Central, and confirmation;
  nested worker fan-out is refused.
- Keep existing Objectives rows, kickoff custody, Scheduler fairness,
  Coordinator/RunServer recovery, steering, cancellation, atomic terminal
  reduction, and delivery ownership. Jido spawn/monitor/await state is
  reconstructible execution state, not durable truth. Reuse the qualified
  DirectAnswer task chain for manager planning rather than adding an unqualified
  default profile. Resolve a frozen Settings-owned plan deadline, retry limit,
  and structural call/token reservation before the first manager call; cap
  manager and DirectAnswer-worker calls to those advertised per-call bounds.
  Registered actions retain their own provider-use budgets rather than an
  invented generic provider-call ledger. Add content-free manager/admission
  facts to the existing conversation action-log diagnostics rather than a new
  table, event family, trace subsystem, or logger. Persist only allow-listed
  result/policy/join/count/review and admitted/rejected/fallback/shadow facts;
  prompts, answers, candidate children, provider payloads, and raw errors are
  excluded from that diagnostic evidence before response signals, traces, or
  SQLite persistence. This does not suppress the ordinary conversation answer.
- Implement red/green focused tests for supplied YAML/quoted/list data,
  implicit independent work, dependent sequences, count mismatch, overflow,
  model failure, repair exhaustion, original-request/effect grounding, worker
  selection, budgets, confirmation, crash/recovery, content-free observability,
  and every surface's use of the central Runtime/Objectives path. Include
  contrastive rule cases for supplied command-shaped data, two self-contained
  analyses with parent-only join guidance, and dependent/effectful work. Run
  warning-free compile, formatter, targeted Credo/Dialyzer/docs/diff checks,
  and an independent authority/drift review. Do not run an aggregate, construct
  a package, or ask for operator validation in this milestone.

Acceptance:

- Ordinary supplied text on the conversational route cannot frame children merely because it contains
  separators, commands, or list syntax; useful single-turn behavior remains.
  At least two independent tasks can be admitted without explicit parallel
  wording, while a dependency or uncertain split remains one turn.
- Both exact counted protocols still work without the planner provider and
  create the same typed durable plan shape. A mismatch or partial parse creates
  no parent or child.
- Planner/worker model output grants no action or permission. The ordinary
  action Adapter and the DirectAnswer-backed Jido worker are both proven through
  one Worker Interface; effectful rows use
  original operator evidence and ordinary confirmation semantics.
- A normal manager decision uses one provider assessment plus local Jido-owned
  adjudication. Its persisted manager/admission facts explain the content-free
  decision and durable framing outcome without retaining prompt, answer, child,
  provider-payload, or raw-error content.
- Focused behavior, recovery, authority, surface, static, docs, and drift checks
  are green at a clean pushed SHA. No aggregate or native package build ran.

##### M9.b.4.1 — Durable typed fan-out provenance remediation

`[code]` `[docs]` `[gate]` `[audit]`

- Add one fan-out-owned persistence codec for the compact plan provenance used
  by both the parent `proposer_hint` and `fanout_proposed` event. The codec owns
  the exact versioned allow-list, canonical encoding, typed decoding, and
  integrity checks. It carries no operator prompt, answer, child result,
  provider payload, credential, or arbitrary metadata. In particular,
  configured/required call and output-token counts must round-trip as bounded
  integers rather than travel through key-name-only secret redaction.
- Keep the generic Security Redactor conservative: numeric or string values
  under arbitrary token-shaped keys remain redacted. Do not add a global
  exemption merely because fan-out has two safe typed counters. Missing,
  additional, string-coerced, negative, redacted-marker, or otherwise corrupt
  budget fields fail closed at the provenance boundary and select a deterministic
  fallback without a composer call. Fallback provenance distinguishes
  `invalid_budget_snapshot` from `deadline_exhausted`; `budget_denied` is not a
  catch-all label for corruption or expiry.
- Prove the actual public path, not only helpers: one Runtime admission frames a
  parent, writes the parent hint and proposal event, reloads them through Repo,
  authorizes the composer from those durable values, terminalizes children,
  invokes the supplied composer exactly once, stores model provenance, and
  delivers the selected report. Add separate recovery and corrupt-budget rows,
  and assert the parent and event contain the same canonical numeric budget.
  Existing in-memory Budget/Composer tests remain useful but cannot satisfy this
  milestone alone.

Acceptance:

- Generic redaction remains fail-closed, while the closed fan-out provenance
  round-trip preserves only its typed safe counters and digests. The full
  Runtime/Repo/Composer test observes one real composition selection from
  rehydrated state; corruption observes zero composer calls and a truthful
  fallback reason.
- Focused owner tests and targeted static/docs/diff checks pass, the change is
  independently reviewed, and no aggregate, package, or operator row runs.

##### M9.b.4.2 — Derived child obligations and semantic completion

`[code]` `[docs]` `[gate]` `[audit]` `[external]`

This milestone established the grounded task contract and RED semantic baseline.
Its single-call reviewer/reviser and receipt-v1 writer were superseded by the
implemented M9.b.4.3 protocol below; they are historical replay context, not an
executable implementation option.

- Add one fan-out worker quality contract derived from the digest-verified
  original request, the compiled child objective, and its expected-result
  guidance. Compose the existing `DirectAnswer.Policy.rule_specs/0` rather than
  copying its factual/direct/no-effect rules, with only a small fan-out-child
  extension for task-scope coverage, explicitly requested dimensions,
  completion preconditions, internal consistency, and unsupported guarantees/
  uncertainty. The same typed contract
  derives provider prompts, closed review evidence, automated scenario rubrics,
  and attended criteria;
  no domain keyword/regex, source formatting, exact sentence, or OTP/event-log
  special case enters production behavior.
- Apply that contract to every newly executed fan-out child whose validated
  action is DirectAnswer and whose source is conversation-manager, exact-
  counted, or verified operator steering. Corrupt/untrusted plan provenance
  cannot manufacture the parent/task binding and therefore fails rather than
  claiming quality completion. Ordinary non-fan-out Objectives and historical
  v1 replay remain unchanged. A validated non-DirectAnswer child stays on the
  existing registered-action Adapter and effect-receipt contract; it neither
  spends a semantic-review call nor fabricates a quality receipt.
- QualityPolicy task-contract/rule-catalog version 2 is local immutable data. Its
  canonical task input has exact top-level keys `version`, `source`,
  `original_request`, `child_objective`, `expected_result`,
  `completion_obligation`, `steering`, and `rules`. `completion_obligation` is
  the exact Allbert-owned object
  `{"version":1,"requirement_sources":["child_objective","expected_result"],
  "satisfaction_policy":"all_explicit_requirements_present_and_supported",
  "missing_required_evidence_outcome":"unresolved"}`. It is derived after the
  existing plan/child binding is verified; FanoutManager/FanoutPlan retain their
  three-field advisory child schema and need no second acceptance-provenance
  format. The exact task-neutral `completion_preconditions` rule requires every
  explicit output named by either requirement source to be present and
  supported; accurately reporting that required evidence is absent is truthful
  but not completion, and the answer must not invent it to force acceptance.
  Its attended criteria are `all_explicit_outputs_present`,
  `required_outputs_supported`, `missing_evidence_is_unresolved`, and
  `no_invention_to_force_completion`. `steering` is `null` for an unchanged
  compiled child; for an operator-
  steered child it has exactly `directive_event_id_sha256` and
  `directive_sha256`, ordinary SHA-256 over the exact unnormalized UTF-8 event id
  and directive. Because steering replaces the child objective without rewriting
  model-authored acceptance criteria, its effective `expected_result` is the
  Allbert-owned task-neutral literal `Complete the operator-steered child task.`
  rather than stale compiled guidance. SHA-256 of that canonical JSON is the
  task-contract binding used by prompt, receipt, tests, and report.
  Locally normalized rule results must appear once in that catalog order; maps
  are recursively string-keyed and lexicographically key-sorted, arrays
  preserve their declared order, and compact UTF-8 JSON has no trailing newline.
  The shared pure
  `AllbertAssist.Objectives.CanonicalJSON` owns that encoding for existing
  fan-out plan/report bindings and the new quality contracts so equivalent
  domains cannot drift. Every new typed caller validates its exact string-key
  schema first; distinct input keys may not collide after stringification.
- The implemented temporary Jido worker owns one bounded phase-separated
  lifecycle: `draft -> initial_critics -> accepted | revise -> final_critics ->
  accepted | unresolved`. The draft remains the registered `DirectAnswer`
  action through Registry, Runner, and Security Central, with action-level model
  failover disabled only for this grounded fan-out-worker context. Exactly two
  transient critics assess disjoint policy-owned rule groups and cannot revise
  text. An all-satisfied first fan-in accepts the unchanged draft; otherwise one
  separately configured generator may revise once and a fresh critic pair judges
  only the revised bytes. Invalid, unavailable, timed-out, violated, or
  unresolved evidence fails honestly. No model-authored aggregate verdict,
  majority vote, free-form critique, or semantic retry loop grants completion.
- A draft is reviewable only when DirectAnswer reports
  `response.direct_answer.source=:model` and one physical provider attempt.
  `Worker.Agent` owns the ephemeral phase counts; the accepted no-revision shape
  is exactly `1/2/0/0/3`, and the accepted revision shape is exactly
  `1/2/1/2/6`. Early failures retain their actually observed count and never
  mint a completion receipt. Every ReqLLM request and enclosing Jido command has
  `max_retries: 0`, so a counted call cannot conceal framework transport retries.
  Ordinary DirectAnswer keeps its existing fallback behavior.
- Freeze implementation ownership instead of spreading a second quality path
  through Runtime. `AllbertAssist.Objectives.Runs.Worker.Grounding` exposes the
  already-digest-verified parent request alongside the compiled task;
  `Worker.QualityPolicy` owns the versioned closed rules/task digest;
  `Worker.QualityReceipt` owns canonical receipt validation;
  `Worker.Commands.Execute` remains the registered-action draft command;
  `Worker.Commands.ReviewRound`, `Worker.Commands.Revise`, the shared
  `Fanout.CriticAgent`, and `Fanout.ReviewRound` own the separate review/revision
  phases; and `Worker.Agent` / `Worker.JidoAdapter` own the allowed transitions
  and deadline/cancellation envelope. The Adapter passes a
  private fan-out-worker no-failover marker to DirectAnswer; DirectAnswer honors
  it only for this grounded child context and returns the private 0/1 call count
  alongside its existing source metadata.
  Child generation/revision resolves the closed `direct_answer` task chain;
  critics resolve `fanout_review`, cross the existing Disclosure boundary, and
  apply the remaining plan deadline/token bound before transport. Neither phase
  adds a registered capability action because advisory review/revision has no
  runtime effect or authority.
  The canonical task contract/digest still binds the immutable rule catalog,
  while a provider projection replaces its rule prose with the catalog version/
  digest and the typed completion object. That projection has exactly `version`,
  `source`, `original_request`, `child_objective`, `expected_result`,
  `completion_obligation`, `steering`, and `rule_catalog`; `rule_catalog` has
  exactly `version=2` and `sha256`, derived over canonical `rules` with the ASCII
  domain `allbert:fanout-worker-quality-rules:v2\0`. `QualityPolicy` supplies all
  rules to the provider system-rule envelope exactly once; neither the draft nor
  review user-data payload can restate them under a conflicting prompt role.
  The shared DirectAnswer policy exposes its existing rule catalog as immutable
  version 1, and Worker receipt replay reconstructs catalog-v1 task digests from
  that pinned version rather than whatever catalog is current in a later build.
- Keep the review non-authoritative. It cannot name or invoke an action, grant
  permission, claim an effect, change identity/status/receipt data, create work,
  or become durable acceptance evidence by itself. The surrounding worker
  Interface deterministically validates the closed schema, source/plan/budget
  binding, call count, answer bound, and allowed transition. The accepted final
  answer must pass one shared durable-summary normalizer at the existing
  2,000-character Objective/Step boundary before review and completion; the
  content-free quality receipt binds the Objective/step, task-contract digest,
  rule-catalog version, reviewer profile/config digest, call count, verdict,
  failed rule ids, and that exact normalized-answer digest. Lifecycle
  `observe` verifies the receipt before completion, so a missing/tampered review
  or persistence slice cannot turn an unchecked draft into a completed child.
  Rejected drafts and critique prose are not persisted. Durable Objectives
  remains the recovery and terminal authority; the ephemeral Jido state adds no
  queue, database, prompt store, or second objective evaluator.
- New `run_completed.payload.quality_receipt` writes use receipt v2. In addition
  to the existing Objective/Step/task/answer bindings, receipt v2 records review-
  protocol v1, exactly two critic groups, group-catalog version/digest, reviewer-
  configuration digest, exact draft/initial-critic/revision/final-critic/provider
  counts, and initial/final/accepted assessment digests. Current-write validation
  requires rule-catalog v2 and the exact current group-catalog digest. Receipt v1
  with catalog v1 or v2 remains byte-exact replay only; it cannot authorize a new
  `run_completed` event. Unknown receipt or catalog versions fail closed, and
  report/recovery readers use the separate replay validator.
  `QualityReceipt` canonicalizes that object and derives the
  separate receipt digest carried by the frozen child envelope.
  Compatibility tests consume literal catalog-v1 task, receipt/event, report,
  and digest vectors captured before the v2 writer; they may not manufacture
  historical evidence by downgrading the current producer's output.
  Task and receipt digests prepend the exact ASCII domains
  `allbert:fanout-worker-quality-task:v2\0` and
  `allbert:fanout-worker-quality-receipt:v2\0` respectively for current writes
  before canonical JSON; the v1 domain remains frozen for replay.
  `objective_id_sha256` and `step_id_sha256` are ordinary SHA-256 over
  the exact, unnormalized UTF-8 database ids; `final_answer_sha256` is ordinary
  SHA-256 over the exact normalized UTF-8 answer;
  `reviewer_config_sha256` uses the task-resolved profile/provider/model and
  effective timeout/output-token bounds plus the closed-evidence transport
  version and exact response-schema digest under
  `allbert:fanout-worker-reviewer-config:v2\0`.
- Extract the current UTF-8 Objective summary redaction/truncation into the pure
  `AllbertAssist.Objectives.ObservationSummary.normalize/1` boundary and use it
  before both review and terminalization. `Lifecycle.observe` validates the
  transient receipt, then the existing `TerminalTransitions.terminalize_child`
  transaction appends that closed receipt in the existing `run_completed` event
  payload while it commits the Step and Objective. The terminal update includes
  the exact transient `state.step.id` in `Objective.current_step_id`; Step
  finalization, event correlation, and any final parent v2 freeze occur in that
  same immediate transaction. Report code may verify but never reconstruct the
  binding. No column, table, event kind,
  Settings key, raw draft, or critique store is added. Recovery/projection must
  verify that durable event before accepting a completed child into a frozen
  report; the frozen child envelope and report-input digest bind its receipt
  digest. For a reviewed DirectAnswer completion, the encoded `run_completed`
  payload has exactly the top-level keys `quality_receipt`, `step_id`, and
  `step_status`; it preserves the existing atomic Step correlation but does not
  duplicate the answer under `summary`. The exact normalized answer remains
  durable on the Objective and Step and remains present in the post-commit
  signal, while the receipt binds its digest. Ordinary/non-reviewed completion payloads retain
  their existing summary shape. Maximum-size and 16-child tests keep the
  existing 2,000-character event-payload changeset bound and assert the complete
  receipt payload itself remains below that limit after JSON encoding, including
  maximal identifiers. Historical pre-contract completed children remain
  readable but cannot be newly selected as quality-reviewed v2 input.
- Prove the real behavior boundary with task-neutral RED/green scenarios: an
  inaccurate restart-strategy draft is repaired; an exactly-once replay
  overclaim is repaired; omitted required nuance that remains unresolved does
  not complete; accurate paraphrases pass; and unrelated domains exercise the
  same rules. Fakes prove every lifecycle branch and budget/recovery invariant;
  a configured real-provider matrix plus agent/operator FOV supplies semantic
  judgment and may not be replaced by response regexes.
- The Worker evaluator records per frozen row only a closed preparation,
  invocation, review-validation, or fixture-expectation stage/reason plus the
  failed-rule count. It does not record answers, provider text, arbitrary
  exceptions, or failed-rule ids.
- Owner tests inspect each critic's emitted raw JSON Schema and require only its
  assigned rule group, `additionalProperties=false`, closed assessment/source-
  handle values, and no provider-owned aggregate verdict or revision field. They
  cover missing/extra/foreign group evidence, invalid source handles, out-of-
  order completion with catalog-order fan-in, no-revision three-call acceptance,
  revised six-call acceptance, final-verification rejection, cancellation/owner
  death, deadline, configuration/catalog/digest tamper, and exact physical-call
  accounting. Captured receipt-v1 and catalog-v1 vectors remain replay-only.
- Add a real two-child Lifecycle integration that executes reviewed
  DirectAnswer workers through terminalization and proves both exact
  `current_step_id`/Step/event bindings plus the final queued layout-v2 parent;
  a real registered-action completion and cancellation/failure rows prove the
  same central binding without hand-editing Objective rows in test setup.

Acceptance:

- A conversation-manager child completes only with one bounded observation bound
  to its compiled task and accepted by two disjoint critics. Revised bytes are
  accepted only after a fresh pair; otherwise the child fails honestly. The exact
  FOV-4 child facts are correct, but production code contains no prompt-specific
  fact list or textual oracle.
- Worker/Jido/action/Runtime recovery owner tests, focused static/docs/diff
  checks, and independent executable review pass at clean pushed `c3baec24`.
  The configured-provider matrix is complete RED evidence: its sole mixed row
  is bounded performance RED at `3bb42119`. Agent and operator FOV remain held;
  no aggregate, package, or full SV runs before attended acceptance.

##### M9.b.4.3 — Phase-separated rule critics and verified worker completion

`[code]` `[docs]` `[gate]` `[audit]` `[external]`

Implementation status: executable behavior, focused owner/recovery tests,
warning/static checks, and independent executable audit are complete at clean
pushed `c3baec24`. The legacy uniform configured-provider row is semantic RED at
`e805045f`; the frozen mixed-topology gate seam landed at `933d604a`, and its
sole real-provider comparison is closed bounded performance RED at `3bb42119`.
Milestone acceptance and attended qualification remain open/held; the system has
returned to the operator without a new remediation path.

- The implemented Worker uses phase-separated advisory work. A DirectAnswer
  worker first produces one immutable draft.
  Exactly two policy-owned, non-empty rule groups cover every current
  `QualityPolicy` rule once. Two private Critic Agents separately assess those
  disjoint groups; neither can generate or revise the answer. Here, separate
  means a distinct provider invocation and tracked Jido lifecycle from the
  generator/reviser. It does not mean a different model, replicated voting, or
  epistemic corroboration. Allbert
  deterministically merges results in catalog order and rejects missing,
  duplicate, extra, wrong-group, malformed, timed-out, or unresolved evidence.
- Version the closed assessment shape additively. Each policy-owned rule
  property returns only `satisfied | violated | unresolved` plus a non-empty
  subset of closed source handles (`task_contract`, `candidate`). Allbert binds
  those handles to whole-source digests and validates group/rule ownership
  locally. Do not ask a model for byte ranges: they add brittle false precision
  without proving semantics. Source handles make the advisory basis inspectable;
  they are not semantic proof or effect evidence. Durable receipts retain only
  assessment/group-catalog, reviewer-configuration, and candidate digests plus
  exact phase counts. Raw task text, candidate text, handles per rule, and critic
  prose remain outside receipts, metrics, traces, and diagnostics.
- Freeze worker group catalog v1 without changing QualityPolicy rule meaning or
  order. `coverage_fidelity` owns `answer_current_request`,
  `supplied_text_is_data`, `preserve_supplied_semantics`,
  `no_unsupplied_details`, `child_task_scope`, `requested_dimensions`, and
  `completion_preconditions`. `safety_consistency` owns
  `memory_is_reference`, `no_false_effect_claims`,
  `no_routing_or_confirmation`, `acknowledgments_are_not_commitments`,
  `useful_factual_and_brief`, `internal_consistency`, and
  `uncertainty_and_guarantees`. The pure compiler proves an exact disjoint cover
  of the existing fourteen-rule catalog.
- When the first fan-in is all satisfied, that review is final for the unchanged
  draft; it is neither rewritten nor reviewed again for ceremony. Otherwise one
  separate configured generator may revise exactly once from the deterministic
  violated/unresolved rule set and the policy-owned instructions. A fresh pair
  of Critic Agents verifies the exact revised bytes. Any remaining violation or
  unresolved assessment terminalizes the child honestly unresolved. The
  reviser never authors its own verdict, and a first-round critic never verifies
  its own revision.
- Use one shared private `CriticAgent` plus pure review-round/assessment modules
  under `AllbertAssist.Objectives.Fanout`; Worker Agent remains the lifecycle
  coordinator. Two owner-linked, monitored Tasks each host one pure CriticAgent
  and execute `Jido.Agent.cmd(..., timeout: 0, max_retries: 0)` directly; the owner receives
  their tagged results, sorts them by policy group rather than completion order,
  and brutally stops any sibling on cancellation, timeout, owner death, or first
  infrastructure failure. Do not use `AgentServer`, `SpawnAgent`,
  `Jido.Await.all/3`, or a supervisor-owned provider task here: those would move
  execution outside the existing killable RunServer/composer Task boundary. No
  critic becomes an Objective, registered capability action, durable queue, or
  supervised service.
- Do not hide the nested-concurrency multiplication. With six globally active
  child runs, a critic phase can produce at most twelve Worker critic
  invocations; the existing single-owner ReportComposer can add one two-critic
  phase, for a runtime maximum of fourteen fan-out-owned critic invocations.
  The milestone adds no second scheduler or concurrency knob, but focused load/
  deadline/cancellation tests and content-free telemetry must prove that bound,
  sibling cleanup, and truthful timeout behavior before real-provider
  qualification.
- Add closed Settings task chains `model_preferences.tasks.fanout_manager` and
  `model_preferences.tasks.fanout_review`. FanoutManager resolves and
  Disclosure-authorizes only `fanout_manager`. Under one resolved Settings
  snapshot, Runtime separately preflights and Disclosure-authorizes the execution
  roles `direct_answer`, `fanout_review`, and `fanout_synthesis` before computing
  Budget v2 or durably framing a parent. Critics resolve only `fanout_review`;
  child drafting/revision remains the registered DirectAnswer task, and report
  generation/revision resolves `fanout_synthesis`. Defaults inherit
  `direct_answer_local` so schema upgrades are additive and existing operators
  are not forced into a new model download. Any missing/unavailable execution
  role before framing selects the existing ordinary DirectAnswer path with one
  content-free exact-role reason and no repeated confirmation.
- Bind each production manager, critic, and synthesis request to the exact
  redacted role/profile/provider/model/effective-endpoint/request/protocol
  configuration used for that attempt. Manager repair binds the ordered attempt-
  set digest; critic-round configuration becomes the receipt/selection
  `reviewer_config_sha256`; synthesis generation/revision configuration is
  validated at the private phase boundary and is deliberately not added to the
  frozen public selection carrier. Injected test clients are labelled fixture
  evidence and cannot mint production-authoritative provenance. Model Doctor reports each
  closed role chain, exact resolved or unavailable role, `auto-pull=false`, and
  only fixed redacted diagnostic codes/messages; it never prints credentials,
  raw provider bodies, arbitrary exceptions, or an unredacted endpoint.
- Introduce Budget v2 as an additive new-write snapshot while preserving exact
  Budget-v1 validation/replay. Its structural call formula is
  `manager_attempts + child_count * 6 + 6`: each potential DirectAnswer child
  reserves one draft, two first-round critics, one possible revision, and two
  post-revision critics; synthesis reserves the same six-call worst case.
  Output-token reservation is
  `manager_attempts * 1024 + child_count * 3072 + 4096`: draft/revision and
  every critic are each capped at 512 tokens, while initial/revised synthesis
  are each capped at 1024. The default eight-child/two-manager-attempt plan
  therefore requires 56 calls and 30,720 tokens. Raise only the existing
  defaults from `40` to `64` calls and from `24,000` to `32,768` tokens; preserve
  explicit operator values and the existing allowed ranges. Lower explicit
  limits continue to reject an oversized plan before durable framing.
- Make the call reservation physically truthful. Update the locked transitive
  ReqLLM `1.17.1` dependency to released `1.18.0`, which contains upstream
  `retry: honor caller retry limits` and passes `user_opts` to
  `ReqLLM.Step.Retry.attach/2`. Set `max_retries: 0` in every fan-out-owned
  manager, child draft/revision, critic, and synthesis generation/revision
  ReqLLM request as well as every enclosing Jido command. The pinned 1.17.1
  code's `Step.Retry.attach/1` defaulted to three transport retries, so a Jido-
  only setting would not close this boundary. After the bounded dependency
  update, one counted provider call means one physical request attempt; no
  framework retry, JSON repair, model fallback, or second adapter call is
  hidden beneath it. Add a retryable transport/action-error regression that
  observes exactly one physical adapter invocation and one terminal failure.
- The existing `300,000` ms setting remains one monotonic hard plan deadline,
  not a promise that a maximum-child plan will finish or a sum of provider
  timeouts. First-round critics run concurrently and are final when the draft is
  unchanged; the second pair runs only after revision. DirectAnswer declares
  automatic retry unsafe once its attempt begins because provider dispatch is
  externally opaque. Coordinator pre-attempt persistence/start recovery remains,
  but a known returned semantic/provider failure terminalizes unresolved and an
  uncertain interrupted dispatch parks or fails closed under the existing
  recovery contract instead of replaying up to six calls. The existing worker-attempt setting
  remains available for actions whose registered retry contract is safe; it no
  longer multiplies DirectAnswer quality-call reservation. There is no semantic
  retry loop, refund, hidden provider use, or new budget knob.
- Bump new worker writes to quality-receipt v2 while retaining unchanged
  QualityPolicy/rule-catalog v2 and adding rule-group-catalog v1. Existing
  receipt-v1/catalog-v1-or-v2 rows retain byte-exact replay semantics but cannot
  authorize a new completion. Receipt v2 extends the existing content-free
  carrier with `review_protocol_version=1`, `critic_group_count=2`,
  `rule_group_catalog_version=1`, `rule_group_catalog_sha256`, exact
  `draft_call_count`/`initial_critic_call_count`/`revision_call_count`/
  `final_critic_call_count`/`provider_call_count`, and
  `initial_assessment_sha256`/nullable `final_assessment_sha256`/
  `accepted_assessment_sha256`; raw
  source-handle assignments and prose remain transient so the completion event stays within its
  existing bound. A no-revision acceptance records `1/2/0/0/3`; a revised
  acceptance records `1/2/1/2/6`. The final Lifecycle boundary still validates
  task/step/objective, answer digest, phase arithmetic, exact provider-call
  count, and receipt integrity before a completion event can exist.
- Do not mutate a nonterminal Budget-v1 fan-out into Budget v2 during upgrade.
  Recovery identifies the immutable framed budget before any provider call.
  Existing terminal children and selected reports retain exact replay; every
  queued, claimed, running, or dispatch-uncertain DirectAnswer child under a v1
  budget transitions idempotently to failed/unresolved with closed reason
  `quality_protocol_upgrade_required` and makes zero new provider calls. A
  joined, queued-composer, composing, or mixed parent that still carries Budget
  v1 selects the existing complete-child deterministic fallback with closed
  reason `review_protocol_upgrade_required`, again with zero provider calls.
  Compare-and-set recovery must tolerate repeats without duplicate terminal
  events, reports, or delivery. There is no in-place budget rebind, retroactive
  receipt minting, or replay of uncertain dispatch; an operator retry creates a
  new Budget-v2 plan while preserving the old record for inspection.
- Extend focused tests with two separately scripted critics, out-of-order
  completion, one-group failure, cancellation/parent death, deadline exhaustion,
  missing/duplicate/foreign source handles and group assignments, no-revision
  acceptance, one-revision
  acceptance, final-verification rejection, budget denial before framing, role-
  profile unavailability, old-receipt replay, every nonterminal Budget-v1
  upgrade state, retryable Jido/ReqLLM failure with exactly one physical
  provider attempt, and content-free observability.
  Tests judge typed rules and scenario outcomes; they do not match domain facts,
  exact prose, or prompt substrings.

Acceptance:

- A new DirectAnswer child completes only after the exact final answer passes a
  complete two-group rule assessment from provider calls separate from its
  generator, bound to its task and candidate digests. Revised bytes require a
  fresh critic pair. No call both revises and judges its own text;
  every failure is visible as unresolved without granting action/effect
  authority.
- Worker/Settings/Jido/Lifecycle owner and recovery tests, focused warning/
  format/Credo/Dialyzer/docs/diff checks, one task-neutral real-provider Worker
  matrix, and independent review pass at one clean pushed SHA. No aggregate,
  package, full SV, or operator row runs before the M9.b.5.3 rejoin.

#### M9.b.5 — Durable main-model fan-in and focused operator qualification

`[code]` `[docs]` `[gate]` `[audit]` `[external]` `[fv]`

- Change terminal reduction so the final child atomically binds the immutable
  ordered child/result/status snapshot with a digest and join outcome, sets
  bounded `report_composition_state=queued`, and leaves
  `report_delivery_state=not_ready`. No surface may build its own joined report
  or deliver an uncomposed transient result.
- Add one recoverable private composer using a closed
  `model_preferences.tasks.fanout_synthesis` chain that initially names the same
  qualified DirectAnswer profile. Its typed input is the original request,
  canonical plan, and bounded child envelopes. Its one structured output is a
  content-free, versioned list of relationship sections with exact keys
  `relationship` and `ordered_queue_positions`; it cannot author report prose,
  change status/outcome, arrange a failed/cancelled/abandoned child, claim an
  effect, invoke an action, or create another fan-out. Sections must exactly
  partition completed children, with a real multi-child relationship required
  when at least two children completed. The deterministic Allbert renderer owns
  every word and renders non-completed children first. Persist the bounded report
  body/source, input digest, and `report_selection_digest` before atomically
  moving delivery to `pending`; the selection digest binds source plus normalized
  model/profile/provider/layout provenance or fallback reason/layout version.
- On unavailable/malformed/timed-out/exhausted composition, persist the existing
  deterministic complete-child renderer as `fallback` and make it deliverable.
  Rehydrate queued composition idempotently; an interrupted `composing` row
  becomes the deterministic fallback at boot, so a crash cannot lose a
  completed child, double-compose/deliver, or strand a joined parent. Existing
  report receipts, notification policy, next-turn delivery, and all surface
  projections consume exactly the stored selected report. A missed advisory
  join signal or TUI fan-out-bus restart re-subscribes and reconciles attached or
  pending reports from durable Objectives/outbox state; it does not trigger a
  second model composition.
- Run focused terminal-race, restart, duplicate-signal, partial/failure,
  cancellation, receipt-truth, redaction/bounds, composer-success, provider-
  failure/fallback, and surface-parity tests plus warning-free targeted static/
  docs/diff checks and an independent drift review. Then run only the numbered
  `FOV-*` attended source qualification in the request flow with a disposable
  Home and real configured model. It proves single-turn supplied data,
  uncounted adaptive planning, concurrent child work, semantic fan-in, counted
  offline admission, deterministic fallback, truthful partial failure, and an
  independent follow-up. It does not run Memory/Search lifecycle FV.
- Classify a focused-operator finding as environment, documentation, or
  executable. Repeat an environment row narrowly; docs-only fixes receive diff
  hygiene plus the docs gate; batch executable findings into one bounded tranche
  with focused owner tests per commit. An executable SHA change restarts the
  focused setup on a fresh Home and repeats only affected/dependent `FOV-*`
  rows. After focused acceptance, close that Home and begin the complete v1.3
  SV-0 through SV-9 source validation from scratch. Do not inherit focused rows
  as full-product evidence.

Acceptance:

- One durable selected report truthfully synthesizes all children on TUI and
  every shared projection; partial/failure status and receipts cannot be erased
  by the model. Every unreceipted child summary is explicitly a child-reported
  observation, not effect evidence. Provider failure yields a complete
  deterministic report rather than a stranded parent, and source/provenance or
  layout/body/input tampering makes the projection fail closed.
- The focused real-provider `FOV-*` row passes at a clean pushed SHA and its
  exact commands/semantic expectations are recorded in the request flow. No
  aggregate, package construction, Memory/Search lifecycle row, or full v1.3
  operator row runs before this focused acceptance.
- After focused acceptance, the complete v1.3 operator validation restarts at
  SV-0 with a new disposable Home. Package construction remains blocked until
  that full source pass is accepted.

##### M9.b.5.1 — Complete bounded child evidence remediation

`[code]` `[docs]` `[gate]` `[audit]`

- Replace the fixed, silent 500-byte child-detail cut with one deterministic
  report-budget policy shared by model and fallback rendering. Freeze complete

  only an over-bound final report may allocate a fair bounded share per child.
  Any shortening must preserve valid UTF-8, stop at a readable boundary, append
  an explicit marker, and state that the complete result remains available from
  the central child Objective. A clipped fragment must never be labelled the
  complete or authoritative child result.
- Preserve one stored report and one projection path. Status totals, ordering,
  relationship membership, failures/cancellations, and exact effect receipts
  remain deterministic and cannot be clipped away. Model composition still
  chooses only the closed layout; it does not rewrite or summarize child facts.
  Healthy authorized composition records source `model`; unavailable,
  malformed, timed-out, or denied composition records the exact fallback source
  and reason. Neither surface presentation nor a larger unbounded body is the
  repair.
- Add independent behavior rows for two children whose `1,000+`-character
  observations contain facts near the end, three heterogeneous children,
  the absolute supported 16-child maximum, multi-byte Unicode, long unbroken input,
  partial/failure/effect-receipt truth, model and fallback rendering, and
  deterministic replay. The two- and three-child rows must retain their complete
  facts when under the report bound. The maximum row must stay within the bound
  and visibly mark every shortened child without invalid UTF-8 or mid-word
  endings.
- Before asking the operator to validate again, run the deterministic
  M9.b.4.1/M9.b.5.1 matrix, warning-free focused static checks, and the opt-in

  the expected content-free manager/admission facts. Inspecting helper output or
  calling manager/composer in isolation is not sufficient.
- Reconcile the literal FOV-0/FOV-1/FOV-2/FOV-4/FOV-7R/FOV-8 commands and
  semantic PASS/FAIL expectations with that clean rehearsal. Record only clean
  SHA, disposable evidence root, counts, closed outcomes, report source, and
  test commands in Build Progress; do not copy prompts, answers, child
  observations, provider payloads, raw action logs, or secrets. Obtain an
  independent post-repair drift audit before handoff. The operator repeats only
  that repaired row on another fresh Home, then starts full SV-0 through SV-9;
  FOV does not re-run supplied-data, counted-protocol, or ordinary-answer rows

Acceptance:

- The exact FOV-4-shaped two-child report retains the OTP restart-strategy and
  event-log idempotent-replay facts, records a healthy model selection, and has
